2025-06-18T02:35:24.4485539Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4485692Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4486318Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4487046Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4487239Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4487394Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4488657Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4489886Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4490016Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4490734Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4491405Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4491848Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4492299Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4493062Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4493517Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4493887Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4494088Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4495015Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4495959Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4496098Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4496312Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4496411Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4496746Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4497018Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4497409Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4498226Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4498872Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4499173Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4499278Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4499574Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4499764Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4499863Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4500083Z ```bash
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4500817Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4501387Z ```
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4501488Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4501577Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4501809Z ```bash
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4502456Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4503192Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4503423Z ```
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4503517Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4503672Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4503925Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4504025Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4504620Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4505149Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4505227Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4505483Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4505987Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4506510Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4507029Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4507620Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4507943Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4508327Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4508811Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4508898Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4509012Z 
2025-06-18T02:35:24.4509015Z
